Since 1984 we have been grilling, frying and sizzling up the best seafood dishes on the Alabama Gulf Coast. Sitting in the heart of Orange Beach, Doc's Seafood Shack offers a gorgeous view of the bay along with excellent food and great service. Are you looking for some of the best tasting seafood on the Gulf Coast? Would you like to dine in an atmosphere that is so casual and laid back that flip flops are encouraged? Are you looking for a Gulf Coast seafood restaurant that is fairly priced and family friendly? Then come on down to Doc's Seafood Shack & Oyster Bar. If you are really hungry, try Doc's "Create Your Own" Seafood Platter. You can choose from several seafood selections and have it fried or grilled. For the more health conscious, try Doc's specialty Baked Seafood Platter. Doc's Seafood Shack serves some of the tastiest seafood dishes on the Gulf Coast and there is something for everyone... even a great Hamburger, amazing Fried Chicken, and homemade Onion Rings. If you and your family are looking for one of the best Gulf Shores seafood restaurants or Orange Beach Seafood restaurant... come on down to Doc's Seafood Shack & Oyster Bar, and don't forget your flip-flops.

body: The service (Dwayne) was great.  The atmosphere was cool.  Old school, love that.  Issue is we had the shrimp (split it) and onion rings.  I have had fried shrimp but this one just seemed a bit greasier.  There was a grease aftertaste.  Nothing big, just aftertaste.  I have had other fried shrimp in this area and did not have that.  True with both the onion rings and shrimp.  They tasted great until you ended up with that aftertaste.
Nice big shrimp so no complaints there.  12 for $26.  Had another issue with the beer.  It came in about a 12 oz tall plastic glass and was warm.  Not hot, just not what beer should be.
The real gotcha was $13.50 for one beer!  Michelob Ultra no less!  Yep, $13.50 for one.  Had a long island which was $10.  Not bad there.  Just did not understand the beer price!  wow.

reviewer_name: Tamara Johnson
rating: 5
body: This is the first review with the original restaurant.   Went to both within three days.  This was the better of the two and wish that I came the second time.
Went for lunch the other day.  Since end of the rush, seated right away but nice flow.
The waitress was very nice and helpful since I never made cocktail sauce showed me and checked to make sure I was good.  Ordered cra  cakes and make your platter.
The true stars was the oysters and shrimp both were fresh and cooked with a perfect touch.  The crab cakes and stuffed crabs were good.  The clam strips were mid,but the shrimp and oysters made up for it.
Also my waitress timed my appetizer and food perfect and kept my ice tea glass full.  Also excellent ice tea.   Not too sweet and enjoyed three glassed.  Average on what seafood cost these days.  There are two locations and highly recommend this one.
